## **19. Post by `@nate.id` on 2026-11-15**

<div class="bsky-card" markdown="1">

<div class="bsky-header" markdown="1">

<img src="https://cdn.bsky.app/img/avatar/plain/did:plc:gxljrt3avermp6gkgkd6xqq5/bafkreiazdkzkksq6ficrizr7ik2uckpvpnm7wq3ixcygv7jg4gcdrvqcxe" class="bsky-avatar">

<div class="bsky-author-meta" markdown="1">
**Nate** [@nate.id](https://bsky.app/profile/did:plc:gxljrt3avermp6gkgkd6xqq5){:target="_blank"}
<br><small>2026-11-15</small>
</div>
</div>

<div class="bsky-content" markdown="1">

I wanted @tidysky.io to ask for as little access as possible, so it only gets 9 narrow permissions: follow/unfollow, lists, mute, and a few reads. No posting, no likes, no DMs. Screenshots are from Bluesky's own sign-in screen. atproto BuildInPublic



<div class="bsky-image-grid bsky-grid-3" markdown="1">



![Bluesky&#39;s sign-in page showing the permissions TidySky requests. Calls to Bluesky&#39;s app service: getProfile, getProfiles, getFollowers, muteActor, unmuteActor. Data: follows (create and delete only), lists and list items (create, update and delete).](https://cdn.bsky.app/img/feed_fullsize/plain/did:plc:gxljrt3avermp6gkgkd6xqq5/bafkreiavbwrnjmvmgcf4abrrcfsucdolftv2avak6vfpijerf5teuunj3m){ .bsky-img }


![Bluesky&#39;s sign-in page showing TidySky&#39;s full permission list: atproto, follow create and delete, list, list item, and five read and mute calls to Bluesky&#39;s app service.](https://cdn.bsky.app/img/feed_fullsize/plain/did:plc:gxljrt3avermp6gkgkd6xqq5/bafkreie4asub3vcqct3xrxahby6mg7ecpp5j6rwadgnewdvxt4aqdn7ci4){ .bsky-img }


![What TidySky can do with your account. Can: know which account you are, follow and unfollow, create and edit lists, add and remove people in lists, mute and unmute, read profiles and your followers. Can&#39;t: post, reply or repost, like posts, delete your posts, read or send DMs, block anyone, or change your profile or settings.](https://cdn.bsky.app/img/feed_fullsize/plain/did:plc:gxljrt3avermp6gkgkd6xqq5/bafkreie672awadbxzoj5nmi5cxmwhklsuwfa4ddo2wylhtmje7vg3kcz6e){ .bsky-img }


</div>





</div>

<p class="link-desc">2026年11月10日から12日にかけて、GitHub 上の多数のリポジトリに対して悪性コミットにより GitHub Actions のワークフローに悪性コードが注入される事例が発生しています。 挿入されたコードは、ワークフローや npm フックが実行されると、CI環境内のシークレットを収集し、C2サーバーへのHTTP通信または Telegram Bot API への送信を試みる infostealer マルウェアです。 本記事は、弊社が Takumi Guard の解析基盤をベースに観測＆分析した情報を踏まえ、日本のコミュニティに向けて、影響と対策を中心に整理するものです。 TL;DR — …</p>
<small>:material-link: https://blog.flatt.tech/entry/github_actions_wi...</small>
</div>
</div>
</a>

## **143. Post by `@jaz.sh` on 2026-11-11**

<div class="bsky-card" markdown="1">

<div class="bsky-header" markdown="1">

<img src="https://cdn.bsky.app/img/avatar/plain/did:plc:q6gjnaw2blty4crticxkmujt/bafkreigio6g2gv3phaprfjukxpwytdyxyic45reldeptypywkt7xrhi3h4" class="bsky-avatar">

<div class="bsky-author-meta" markdown="1">

<br><small>2026-11-11</small>
</div>
</div>

<div class="bsky-content" markdown="1">

I cooked up a new Graph-ish DB for atproto loosely based on some of my prior blog posts and an internal graph DB I built while I was on the bsky team that supports "Known Followers" in the bluesky app. It's called "marco" and you can explore it here marco.jazco.dev







<a href="https://marco.jazco.dev/" class="bsky-link-preview-wrapper" target="_blank">
<div class="bsky-link-preview" markdown="1">

<img src="https://cdn.bsky.app/img/feed_thumbnail/plain/did:plc:q6gjnaw2blty4crticxkmujt/bafkreia7fn6r5rz4bsfgd4wasinltuldxltrifrs4yhqlcexosss7k36tq" class="link-thumb">
